Abductive move: the body formerly lowered or exhausted is now given a raised support. Reading change: the ayah reads as reversal of `خاشعة`/`ناصبة`: no longer lowered by toil, the body is upheld.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B001","root_000582/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2aeb3cebf47f1176bc47","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:13:3\",\"root_000582\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Aynı yükseltme sözünün daha sonra gök için kullanılması, sedirleri kozmik yüksekliğin insan ölçeğindeki kullanılabilir karşılığı yapar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_39113f362fb0c8e36d0d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:13:3\",\"root_000582\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yükseltme bir haberi duyurur gibi, boş sözün olmadığı yerde bile onurlu kabulü görünür ve meşru biçimde ilan eder.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000697/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3ce5d7a23e56933c7d1f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:13:2\",\"root_000697\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sedirlerin içte saklananı da çağrıştırması, sessiz ve örtülü bahçedeki dinlenme yerlerine kamusal müdahaleden korunmuş bir mahremiyet ekler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8cfa93170df8fb3eff5d","text":"88:14-16 first clarified `ر ف ع` B004 as presentation: the raised place remains usable because other objects are placed, aligned, and spread around it. 88:18 then supplied the decisive surprise by repeating `ر ف ع` for the sky, turning a couch into a domestic-scale analogue of cosmic elevation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B001","root_000697/B010","root_000697/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_90b800fc2e5f3381c616","text":"1. **Raised couches convert inward joy into bodily supported rest.** **Lexical:** `س ر ر` B011, \"place of settled support,\" and B010, \"joy and ease\"; `ر ف ع` B001, physical raising. **Cue:** the couches follow soft faces, satisfaction, a high garden, quiet, and flowing water in 88:8-12. **Role:** the support branch gives the body a stable resting place, the joy branch names its affective correlate, and elevation distinguishes this rest from collapse or depletion. **Abduction:** I infer that supported posture helps produce or express the preceding ease. **Reading change:** `سرر` are not decorative furniture; they are the bodily mechanism by which the habitat's inward ease becomes a settled posture.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000697/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_93dea2bbe171fed87677","text":"2. **Inner joy made into furniture.** Evidence: `س ر ر` B010 joy/ease, B001 hidden inward matter; `ر ض و` B001 satisfaction in 88:9. Structural cue: satisfaction precedes couches; garden objects follow. Role: the couch materializes inner joy as a place to rest. Abductive move: eschatological furnishings externalize states of the self. Reading change: `سرر` carries joy and secrecy as well as literal seats.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9ec9a34cde628a896781","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:13:3\",\"root_000582\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yükseltme aynı zamanda sunma işlevi görür; sedirleri erişimden uzaklaştırmak yerine çevresindeki kap ve yastıklarla birlikte kullanıma hazır biçimde öne çıkarır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000697/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c8e1311679184b37fae7","text":"4. **Concealment becomes privacy rather than deprivation.** **Lexical:** `س ر ر` B001, keeping something hidden inwardly, activated beside B011 support. **Cue:** the couches occur inside the covered garden after the exclusion of vain sound in 88:11. **Role:** the secret/inward branch gives settled support a protected interior quality. **Abduction:** I infer privacy from enclosure plus acoustic peace; no partition is named. **Reading change:** the resting places can be read as secure inward spaces where repose is protected from public intrusion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cc8f4e09517f759b84d2","text":"3. **Elevation presents the resting place rather than withdrawing it.** **Lexical:** `ر ف ع` B004, \"bringing near or presenting,\" alongside B001. **Cue:** the raised couches are immediately followed by placed cups, rowed cushions, and spread carpets in 88:14-16. **Role:** B004 turns raising into presentation within a service arrangement; surrounding placement keeps elevated honor accessible. **Abduction:** I infer that the furnishings are staged for occupants rather than merely stored. **Reading change:** `مرفوعة` can signal readiness and presentation: the couches are raised into available prominence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_de24df12a1b4a0fcc488","text":"The earlier lowered faces and later raised sky formed a vertical arc around the couches. The auditory purification in 88:11 made `ر ف ع` B005 safer to preserve: raised mention is possible only after vain speech is excluded.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B001","root_000582/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e4860e608e321e434bd8","text":"2. **The local furnishing is a human-scale analogue of the raised sky.** **Lexical:** `ر ف ع` B001, raising something upward, and B002, high rank. **Cue:** the exact root recurs in passive form at 88:18, `ٱلسَّمَآءِ كَيْفَ رُفِعَتْ`, while 88:10 has already called the garden high. **Role:** the couch gives elevation a usable domestic scale; the sky gives it a cosmic scale; B002 lets both carry honor as well as geometry. **Abduction:** I infer that root repetition asks the reader to compare the two levels of construction. **Reading change:** raised seating becomes a local participation in a larger vertical order, not an isolated luxury.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e8e5a25b9270d3b59d36","text":"3. **Raised report/publicity without vain speech.** Evidence: `ر ف ع` B005 announcing/spreading a report; `س م ع` B005 reputation/public circulation; `ل غ و` B002 vain speech excluded. Structural cue: sound is purified before objects are raised. Role: elevation includes lawful publicity, not noisy vanity. Abductive move: a place without false talk can still have honorable mention. Reading change: `مرفوعة` also signals exalted recognition.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B001","root_000697/B010","root_000697/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f124001f9320c3edc370","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:13:2\",\"root_000697\",[\"B010\",\"B011\"]],[\"88:13:3\",\"root_000582\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sedirler iç rahatlığını bedeni taşıyan yerleşik desteğe dönüştürür; önce alçalmış ve yorulmuş beden artık düşmek yerine yukarıda tutulur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
